(function() {
  let trackingNumbers = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
    let number = parseInt(trackingNumbers[0].match(/(\d+)/)[0]) - i;
    let prefix = trackingNumbers[0].match(/(\w+)/)[1];
    trackingNumbers.push(`${prefix}${number.toString().padStart(8, '0')}`);
  }
  trackingNumbers.reverse(); // Ensure the numbers are in descending order

  let currentIndex = 0;
  let results = [];
  let batchSize = 10;

  function sendBatchRequest() {
    let batch = trackingNumbers.slice(currentIndex, currentIndex + batchSize);
    return Promise.all(batch.map(sendRequest))
      .then(() => {
        currentIndex += batchSize;
        if (currentIndex < trackingNumbers.length) {
          return sendBatchRequest();
        } else {
          downloadResults();
        }
      })
      .catch(error => {
        console.error("Error in batch processing:", error);
        alert("An error occurred during processing. Please check the console.");
      });
  }

  function sendRequest(trackingNumber) {
    return fetchTrackingData(trackingNumber)
      .then(resultText => {
        results.push({ trackingNumber, result: resultText });
      })
      .catch(error => {
        console.error("Error processing tracking number:", trackingNumber, error);
      });
  }

  function fetchTrackingData(trackingNumber) {
    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append("__EVENTTARGET", "");
    formData.append("__EVENTARGUMENT", "");
    formData.append("__VIEWSTATE", "zaG7GOQMcTfTlvzOklwySpbiP0aZWjVfkFdFs+MUa9lSsp/ZFehEl3dCENSjKg5ovYRbgHZTSjezssDvRBcCKqr9409HnAi6lNAdXfG6mhmkq1eri31YNu+/MfOZ+E+0Fs75wzL5xlbdIeI7uA6FNqeEoUQ=");
    formData.append("Testing", "");
    formData.append("__LASTFOCUSID", "FindBtn");
    formData.append("LoginNameTextBox", "");
    formData.append("PasswordTextBox", "");
    formData.append("ShipmentHousebillNumberTextbox", trackingNumber);
    formData.append("ContainerNumberTextBox", "");
    formData.append("FindBtn", "Find");
    formData.append("__VIEWSTATEGENERATOR", "1806D926");
    formData.append("__SCROLLPOSITIONX", "0");
    formData.append("__SCROLLPOSITIONY", "0");
    formData.append("__VIEWSTATEENCRYPTED", "");
    formData.append("__EVENTVALIDATION", "+eOaASlyoGoCNFKGsXgEA8S9CciE12RfkKmKkEfGIazBDDRSzEAY10bsdFD1gnZcYU0W4OXB0Uuf4pKrjk/aJiCw+RC3F46HLREfXezkPRGYRSPAzmQ0QcxvQdh0I6/70c0sh7lTmXufvxpsIPvHX36zZjxWrxYAJFNJZ1aAB9nV0eBzLesESYr+Wr/Gu5osc47OT7sh8VEHWwsdIa3aLosAYPBj5W0ohqkr+AYIv6VR1SJm2+883d4fZtg3j6UUJ2+OJVek8kvBAVQa+C6bSouhfR0490jv2CsJ1a6VNJhOcZe53T2O+ACSaSfYOPXN15rjYA==");

    return fetch("https://mcehst.webtracker.wisegrid.net/Login/Login.aspx?ReturnUrl=%2fDefault.aspx", {
      method: "POST",
      body: formData,
      headers: {
        "accept": "text/html,application/xhtml+xml,application/xml;q=0.9,image/avif,image/webp,image/apng,*/*;q=0.8,application/signed-exchange;v=b3;q=0.7",
        "accept-language": "en-AU,en-GB;q=0.9,en-US;q=0.8,en;q=0.7",
        "cache-control": "no-cache",
        "pragma": "no-cache",
        "sec-ch-ua": "\"Not-A.Brand\";v=\"99\", \"Chromium\";v=\"124\"",
        "sec-ch-ua-mobile": "?1",
        "sec-ch-ua-platform": "\"Android\"",
        "sec-fetch-dest": "document",
        "sec-fetch-mode": "navigate",
        "sec-fetch-site": "same-origin",
        "sec-fetch-user": "?1",
        "upgrade-insecure-requests": "1"
      },
      referrer: "https://mcehst.webtracker.wisegrid.net/Login/Login.aspx?ReturnUrl=%2fDefault.aspx",
      referrerPolicy: "strict-origin-when-cross-origin",
      mode: "cors",
      credentials: "include"
    })
    .then(response => response.text())
    .then(data => {
      const parser = new DOMParser();
      const doc = parser.parseFromString(data, "text/html");
      return doc.querySelector("body").innerText.trim();
    });
  }

  function downloadResults() {
    let output = "Tracking Number | Result\n";
    results.forEach(item => {
      output += `${item.trackingNumber} | ${item.result}\n`;
    });

    const blob = new Blob([output], { type: "text/plain" });
    const link = document.createElement("a");
    link.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    link.download = "tracking_results.txt";
    link.click();
  }

  // Start processing tracking numbers in batches
  sendBatchRequest();
})();
